==========================================================================================

HRS 2020 Final Release

Note to Users: This codebook is designed to match the distribution dataset. 
Codebook metadata are derived from the data collection instrument.
If you have questions concerning codebook layout or content please bring
them to the attention of the HRS staff at hrsquestions@umich.edu.

Printing recommendation: Set margins (left/right/top/bottom) to .5 inch; print
in portrait orientation using a mono-space 10-point font.

==========================================================================================

RJ005M1                       CURRENT JOB STATUS- 1
         Section: J     Level: Respondent      Type: Numeric    Width: 2   Decimals: 0
         Ref: SecJ.WORKSTATUS.J005MCurrEmpStatus

         Now we're going to ask you some questions about your current employment
         situation.
         
         Are you working now, temporarily laid off, unemployed and looking for work,
         disabled and unable to work, retired, a homemaker, or what?
         
         INSTR: Do not probe but select all that apply
         
         INSTR: If the respondent sometimes engages in casual work, For example, R works
         at the polls during election time, or works some weeks out of the year but is
         not employed at the time of the survey, then the respondent is not employed.
         		
         WEB-ADMINISTERED TEXT:
         Now we're going to ask you some questions about your current employment
         situation.
                 
         Are you working now, temporarily laid off, unemployed and looking for work,
         disabled and  unable to work, retired, a homemaker, or what?
         
         INSTR: Select all that apply.
         
         INSTR: If you sometimes engage in casual work, for example, you work at the
         polls during election time, occasionally do odd jobs, or work some weeks out of
         the year but are not employed at the time of the survey. We do not consider this
         working now.

         IF IWType <> EXT THEN 
         IF WORKINGNOW IN SecJ.WORKSTATUS.J005MCurrEmpStatus OR SecJ.WORKSTATUS.J010_ = 
         YES THEN 
         ASSIGN: SecJ.WORKSTATUS.J020_WorkforPay := 'YES' 
         IF IWType <> EXT THEN 
         IF SecA.Relations.A028_RInNHome = YES AND 
         SecA.HousingLocation.NursingHome.A070_NHOwnRent = NO AND RVARS.Z123_CurWorkng_V 
         <> YES THEN 
         ASSIGN: SecJ.WORKSTATUS.J020_WorkforPay := 'NO' 
         IF IWType <> EXT THEN 
         IF NOT(piSecARelationsA028_RInNHome = YES AND 
         piSecAHousingLocationNursingHomeA070_NHOwnRent = NO AND piRVARSZ123_CurWorkng_V 
         <> YES) THEN 

         - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
RJ020                         WORKING FOR PAY
         Section: J     Level: Respondent      Type: Numeric    Width: 2   Decimals: 0
         Ref: SecJ.WORKSTATUS.J020_WorkforPay

         Are you doing any work for pay at the present time?
         
         INSTR: If the respondent sometimes engages in casual work, for example, R works
         at the polls during election time, occasionally does odd jobs, or works some
         weeks out of the year but is not employed at the time of the survey, then the
         respondent is not employed.
         
         
         WEB-ADMINISTERED TEXT:
         Are you doing any work for pay at the present time?
         
         If you sometimes engage in casual work, for example, you work at the polls
         during election time, occasionally do odd jobs, or work some weeks out of the
         year but are not employed at the time of the survey, we do not consider this
         working now.

         ASSIGN: SecJ.SECONDJOB.J558_ := DontKnow 
         {PREVIOUS ASK} SecJ.SECONDJOB.DolCentAmount_J558_.W509_AmountDollars 
         IF SecJ.SECONDJOB.DolCentAmount_J558_.W509_AmountDollars = Refusal THEN 
         ASSIGN: SecJ.SECONDJOB.J558_ := Refusal 
         {PREVIOUS ASK} SecJ.SECONDJOB.DolCentAmount_J558_.W509_AmountDollars 
         IF NOT(W509_AmountDollars = Refusal) THEN 
         IF SecJ.SECONDJOB.DolCentAmount_J558_.W510_AmountCents = Empty OR 
         SecJ.SECONDJOB.DolCentAmount_J558_.W510_AmountCents = 0 THEN 
         ASSIGN: SecJ.SECONDJOB.J558_ := 
         Val((ToString(SecJ.SECONDJOB.DolCentAmount_J558_.W509_AmountDollars ) + '.00')) 
         {PREVIOUS ASK} SecJ.SECONDJOB.DolCentAmount_J558_.W509_AmountDollars 
         IF NOT(W509_AmountDollars = Refusal) THEN 
         IF SecJ.SECONDJOB.DolCentAmount_J558_.W510_AmountCents > 0 AND 
         SecJ.SECONDJOB.DolCentAmount_J558_.W510_AmountCents < 10 THEN 
         ASSIGN: SecJ.SECONDJOB.J558_ := 
         Val(((ToString(SecJ.SECONDJOB.DolCentAmount_J558_.W509_AmountDollars ) + '.0') 
         + ToString(SecJ.SECONDJOB.DolCentAmount_J558_.W510_AmountCents ))) 
         {PREVIOUS ASK} SecJ.SECONDJOB.DolCentAmount_J558_.W509_AmountDollars 
         IF NOT(W509_AmountDollars = Refusal) THEN 
         IF NOT(W510_AmountCents > 0 AND W510_AmountCents < 10) THEN 
         ASSIGN: SecJ.SECONDJOB.J558_ := 
         Val(((ToString(SecJ.SECONDJOB.DolCentAmount_J558_.W509_AmountDollars ) + '.') + 
         ToString(SecJ.SECONDJOB.DolCentAmount_J558_.W510_AmountCents ))) 
         {PREVIOUS ASK} SecJ.SECONDJOB.J556_

         {PREVIOUS ASK} SecJ.JOBHISTORY.L099_

         ASSIGN: SecJ.JOBHISTORY.LONGESTHELDJOB.L012_YrMostRecJob := 
         MinusInt(Year(SysDate) + SecJ.JOBHISTORY.LONGESTHELDJOB.L010_YrStrtMostRecJob ) 
         {PREVIOUS ASK} SecJ.JOBHISTORY.L099_ 
         IF SecJ.JOBHISTORY.LONGESTHELDJOB.L011_AgeStrtMostRecJo <> Empty AND 
         SecA.ContinuInterview.A019_RAge > 0 THEN 
         ASSIGN: SecJ.JOBHISTORY.LONGESTHELDJOB.L012_YrMostRecJob := 
         MinusInt(Year(SysDate) + MinusInt(SecA.ContinuInterview.A019_RAge + 
         SecJ.JOBHISTORY.LONGESTHELDJOB.L011_AgeStrtMostRecJo )) 
         {PREVIOUS ASK} SecJ.JOBHISTORY.L099_ 
         IF NOT(L011_AgeStrtMostRecJo <> Empty AND piSecAContinuInterviewA019_Rage > 0) 
         THEN 
         ASSIGN: SecJ.JOBHISTORY.LONGESTHELDJOB.L012_YrMostRecJob := '0'

RJ848                         FIRM/UNION OFFERS PENSION
         Section: J     Level: Respondent      Type: Numeric    Width: 2   Decimals: 0
         Ref: SecJ2.J848_

         [Does your current business or union/Does your current employer or union] offer
         retirement accounts, tax deferred retirement savings plans, or pensions to any
         of its employees?
         
         INSTR: If R mentions Social Security, say: We will ask about Social Security
         later in the interview. Here, I just want to find out about other plans operated
